- **rough-vol-forecast**: Rough-volatility-scaled vol forecast (Bayer-Friz-Gatheral 2016) for a ticker across multiple horizons. Under rough vol, realized vol scales as h^H with H around 0.14 empirically (Livieri et al. 2018), much slower than the sqrt(t) growth of Brownian motion. This dampens long-horizon extrapolation and lifts short-horizon estimates. Reports the rough-vol forecast alongside traditional Brownian scaling and EWMA for direct comparison at each horizon. Requires Stocks Basic. Runs on the free tier. ([rgourley/quant-garage](https://github.com/rgourley/quant-garage/tree/HEAD/skills/rough-vol-forecast) · ★7)

Invoke to drain a queue unattended, or to find out what is actually blocking.
